BSE Sensex's big daily moves (±2% or more), 2000–2026: what was in the sky ±3 days

COMEX ^BSESN, 20 Nov 2000 – 20 Nov 2026: 605 days with a close-to-close move of 2% or more out of 6407 (301 up, 304 down; 49.8% up). For each factor, the number of big days on which it was present (events within ±3 calendar days; states on the day; sensex rules active) is compared with the number expected from the same years (big moves cluster in volatile years, so a slow planet that happened to sit in one sign during 2008 or 2013 is not credited). Lift = observed ÷ expected; p = Poisson; q = false-discovery-rate correction over all 698 factors tested — only q < 0.10 should be read as a finding. Big days come in bursts (e.g. March 2020, January 2026), so the episode column shows how many separate occasions stand behind each count; a lift carried by two or three episodes is weaker than the p-value suggests.

Result

  • Any big move (q < 0.10): Rule: Mars With Saturn Rahu in Bullish signs [Bullish] (×1.64); State: Venus retrograde (×1.61); Rule: Mars-Saturn conjunction (same sign) (Strong Bullish Permutation Combination) [Bullish] (×1.56); Rule: Mars-Saturn conjunction (same sign) [Bullish] (×1.56); Rule: Venus-Herschel conjunction (same sign) (Strong Bullish Short Term Yoga'S) [Bullish] (×1.53); Rule: Venus-Herschel conjunction (same sign) [Mixed] (×1.53); State: Mars with Saturn (×1.51); Rule: Venus in Pisces [Bearish] (×1.44); Rule: Venus-Rahu 3/11 (Trikadash) [Bullish] (×1.38); Rule: Mars-Herschel 5/9 (Trine) [Bullish] (×1.31)
  • Big up moves (q < 0.10): none
  • Big down moves (q < 0.10): none

Does it hold out of sample?

Leads picked on 2013 and earlier (lift > 1, p < 0.05 on a side, at least 5 separate episodes) and then measured on the later years: 52 of 95 stayed above 1; pooled 1231 observed against 1124.7 expected (×1.09, p = 0.001).
